# Agents · I · part 16

991 entries, ipc-contract-auditor to issue-tracker. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ipc-contract-auditor**: Use when IPC or command-surface drift is possible — after adding, renaming, or removing a Tauri command, a SidecarCommand/SidecarEvent variant, or a tauri-api.ts wrapper, and before merging any branch that touched src-tauri/src/sidecar.rs, src-tauri/sidecar-opencode/src/types.ts, src-tauri/src/lib.rs, or src/lib/tauri-api.ts. Also use when a command or event silently does nothing at runtime. ([kevinlin/cowork-z](https://github.com/kevinlin/cowork-z/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipc-contract-auditor.md) · ★13)
- **ipc-contract-guardian**: Use this agent whenever a change adds or modifies a Tauri command or a data shape that crosses the Rust↔TypeScript boundary — a new `#[tauri::command]`, a changed field on `Session`/`Problem`/`MutationResult`/an input struct, or a renamed command string. It keeps the four-sided IPC contract in lockstep (Rust struct + serde ⇄ `invoke_handler!` registration ⇄ `src/lib/types.ts` ⇄ `src/lib/tauri.ts` real + mock branches) since nothing in the build enforces this and drift fails silently at runtime. Proactively invoke it after backend command work or before shipping any change that touches the IPC… ([cuevasrja/git-sessions-ui](https://github.com/cuevasrja/git-sessions-ui/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipc-contract-guardian.md))
- **ipc-debugger**: Debug and verify Unix socket IPC layer between yasume GUI and yasume-ctl CLI. ([nwiizo/workspace_2026](https://github.com/nwiizo/workspace_2026/blob/HEAD/samples/pomodoro-timer-rs/.claude/agents/ipc-debugger.md) · ★42)
- **ipc-designer**: Use when designing or extending the Bestfriend IPC contract: adding new window.api.* endpoints, defining typed IPC channels, designing the preload bridge, streaming job-event patterns, or auditing IPC security. Invoke when the renderer needs new data or actions not yet exposed through the IPC bridge. ([GustavsIljuconoks/bestfriend](https://github.com/GustavsIljuconoks/bestfriend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipc-designer.md))
- **ipc-designer**: Designs and evolves the protocol between the vigile daemon and its clients (CLI, menubar) — NDJSON framing, envelopes, event streams, compatibility. Call before adding a command or changing an existing field. ([kevinbarfleur/vigile](https://github.com/kevinbarfleur/vigile/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipc-designer.md))
- **ipc-multicore**: CPU0（Cortex-M85）とCPU1（Cortex-M33）間のコア間通信（共有メモリ、セマフォ、メッセージング、CPU1起動）を実装する。S-006のIssueに使用する。 ([grace2riku/mimamori-sense](https://github.com/grace2riku/mimamori-sense/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipc-multicore.md))
- **ipc-protocol**: Guardián del contrato IPC Java↔Python de CryptoApp. Úsalo para cualquier cambio en el framing MessagePack, los tipos de mensaje, el protocolo de línea en tiempo real (SIGNAL/HEARTBEAT), el ciclo de vida de procesos Python, o para depurar frames corruptos, cuelgues y señales que no llegan. ([alexlp04/cryptoApp](https://github.com/alexlp04/cryptoApp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipc-protocol.md) · ★2)
- **ipc-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for changes that cross the Rust↔TypeScript IPC boundary in this Tauri app — new or changed commands, events, serde types, or plugin API surface. Use proactively after edits touching both src-tauri/src and src/ts, or before committing IPC changes. ([DotMatrixLabs/dot-x](https://github.com/DotMatrixLabs/dot-x/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipc-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **ipc-reviewer**: Read-only review of EnvVault IPC changes (channels, schemas, handlers, preload, renderer calls). Launched by /ev-review when src/shared, schemas, handlers, ipc, preload or window.envvault call sites change. ([msywensky/envvault](https://github.com/msywensky/envvault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipc-reviewer.md))
- **ipc-security-auditor**: Read-only auditor for santree-app's IPC boundary and terminal-compliance invariants. - **ipc-security-reviewer**: Audits the Electron IPC surface (overlay/main.js handlers + overlay/preload.js contextBridge) for path traversal, missing validation, AppleScript injection, and over-broad renderer privileges. ([tatashbar/Economic-Modeling-Studio](https://github.com/tatashbar/Economic-Modeling-Studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ipf-micro-specialist.md) · ★4)

- **iphone-duo**: The foldable iPhone: display facts, the pose matrix, and the shell rules that follow. Pose board link in `local-plan/iphone-duo.md`. - **iphone-duo-adopter**: Takes a Flutter app to full iPhone Duo support, step by step. Interviews the developer about the project, saves the answers to .iphone-duo/profile.md, then runs the eight iphone-duo step skills in order (Flutter upgrade, UIScene plugin audit, UIScene migration, iOS 27.1 toolchain, responsive layout, life cycle and media, hinge detection, hinge avoidance), verifying each one and asking for approval before the next. Run it as the main session (`claude --agent iphone-duo:iphone-duo-adopter`) so it can ask questions; use /iphone-duo:adopt from an existing session. ([alvaroarmijos/iphone-duo-plugin](https://github.com/alvaroarmijos/iphone-duo-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/iphone-duo/agents/iphone-duo-adopter.md))
- **iphone-duo-audit**: Read-only iPhone Duo readiness audit for an iOS codebase. Runs the grep checklist from the iphone-duo skill (UIScreen.main, cached screen widths, orientation/idiom checks, symmetric safe-area math, custom bars, missing UIScene lifecycle, Info.plist keys, camera) and returns a severity-ranked report with file:line and fix pointers. Use when asked whether an app is ready for iPhone Duo / foldable iPhone, or before adapting layouts for Duo. Never edits code. ([anatoliykant/iphone-duo-plugin](https://github.com/anatoliykant/iphone-duo-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/iphone-duo-audit.md) · ★1)

- **iphone-layout**: Use após QUALQUER mudança de interface (telas, menus, batalha, Novidades, web/shell.html) para adaptar e validar o jogo em TODOS os modelos de iPhone do PWA.
